## _t_diag_noprotect residual — ROOT CAUSE refined (2026-07-26)
The 720x486 fix (61d996d) resolved the dim mismatch; the remaining ~19 dB divergence is now understood:
the minimized scenenode's <clip> had its <pathURL> STRIPPED (empty clip) and its Type/Drop-Zone binding
stripped. With no bound media, FCP resolves the drop-zone to one of the transition's BUNDLED Media/
placeholder photos (Diagonal ships Blue_Water/Chocolate/Maple/Plum/Rose/Snow/... — the FCP content
region color [178,149,158] matches NONE of the harness A [140,91,60] / B [100,116,147], and has real
image structure std~50), placed at a default small centered ~284x257 well. The ENGINE instead injects
the harness A full-frame. So this is the "empty-clip / unbound drop-zone -> FCP bundled-Media
placeholder" case (exactly what the removed media-ref + drop-zone-binding protections used to hide).
Matching it requires the engine to (a) detect an unbound/empty-clip drop-zone and (b) resolve + place
FCP's bundled placeholder at its default well size — low value (no shipped transition has an empty
clip). DEFERRED with this decode; not force-fixed. The dims half (720x486) IS shipped + correct.

## DECODE-BLOCKER: FCP no-sceneSettings default canvas is an obscure content-extent heuristic (2026-07-26)
After removing the scene-geometry minimizer protection, re-minimizes strip <sceneSettings> and hit
FCP's no-format default-canvas behavior. Extensive FCP-headless probing (fct _headless-frame on
synthetic Shape docs) shows the default is NOT a simple rule:
  • Image/drop-zone node (uuid 66fc0d6a…), no ss -> 720x486 (even bare).
  • Shape (uuid 712462a4…), no ss -> depends on VERTEX EXTENT/DISTRIBUTION:
      - tri x[200,400] y[200,500]                    -> 720x486
      - x=-384,y=-100                                -> 720x486
      - x=0,   y=-571 (one-sided vertical)           -> 720x486
      - x=-384,y=-544 (one-sided)                    -> 720x486
      - x=-384,y=-571 (one-sided)                    -> 1920x1080
      - y[0,-486] x384 / y[0,-488] x384 (one-sided)  -> 720x486
      - y[+285,-286] x384 (CENTERED, span 571)       -> 1920x1080
  So neither max|coord|, span, nor one-axis extent explains it; the 720->1920 flip depends on a
  COMBINATION of X and Y extent AND whether the shape straddles the origin (multi-quadrant). Looks like
  FCP picks the smallest standard preset (720x486 vs 1920x1080) whose CENTERED frame contains the
  shape's bbox, but the exact metric isn't pinned (candidates: diagonal ~666->688 threshold; bbox must
  fit within ±360x±243 half-extents when centered — a centered 571-tall shape needs >486 so bumps to
  1080, while a one-sided 571 shape is measured differently). NOT cleanly decoded -> NOT implemented
  (RULE 2.0: no curve-fit). Parser keeps the historical 1920x1080 default.
  IMPACT: affects ONLY degenerate minimized repros (all 65 shipped transitions have complete
  sceneSettings). But it DOES mean re-minimizes that strip sceneSettings surface this corner instead of
  the target bug. MITIGATION for the loop: prefer minimizing with the target's real sceneSettings intact
  (the divergence of interest is usually independent of canvas); if a re-minimize collapses onto the
  no-ss canvas corner, that repro is this known blocker — set it aside and target a frame/subtree whose
  divergence survives WITH sceneSettings. Full decode deferred (low value: no shipped transition hits it).

## no-sceneSettings default-canvas: PRECISE thresholds measured (2026-07-26)
Pinned the 720x486 -> 1920x1080 bump with a CENTERED rect (Shape), no sceneSettings, via
fct _headless-frame probes:
  • X: content half-width 375 -> stays 720x486; 376 -> 1920x1080. (content width <= 750)
  • Y: content half-height 278 -> 720x486; 280 -> 1920x1080 (boundary ~279). (content height <= ~558)
So FCP keeps the 720x486 default while the CENTERED content bbox fits within ~750 x ~558 (aspect
~1.344), else bumps to 1920x1080. Also: an Image/drop-zone node (uuid 66fc0d6a) with no sceneSettings
-> 720x486 regardless (its media well is small). NOTE the ~750x558 fit-box is NOT a clean multiple of
720x486 (750/720=1.042, 558/486=1.148 — non-uniform), so no first-principles rule is derived yet and
shipping the raw 750x558 threshold would be a curve-fit (RULE 2.0 forbids). LEFT UNIMPLEMENTED; engine
keeps the historical 1920x1080 default.

WORKFLOW IMPACT + MITIGATION: with scene-geometry stripping now enabled, re-minimizes of transitions
whose bug is canvas-independent (Reflection, Center, Diagonal) collapse onto this no-sceneSettings
canvas corner (FCP 720x486 vs engine 1920x1080) and stop being useful for the target bug. Since ALL 65
shipped transitions ship a COMPLETE sceneSettings block, the productive path is to diagnose against the
EXISTING minimized cases (which retain sceneSettings) or the FULL transition, not the sceneSettings-
stripped re-minimize. Full canvas-rule decode deferred (0 shipped transitions affected).

## no-sceneSettings default canvas — FULLY DECODED as content-bbox-span rule (2026-07-26)
Nailed it with position-independent rect probes (fct _headless-frame): FCP's default canvas when the
scene has no complete format is chosen by the CONTENT BOUNDING-BOX SPAN (width & height of all content,
INDEPENDENT of position — a 100-wide rect offset to x=800 stays 720x486; a 752-wide rect anywhere bumps):
  • bbox width span <= 750  AND  height span <= 558  ->  720x486 (NTSC-DV default)
  • either span exceeds (>750 or >558)              ->  1920x1080
  Exact boundaries: width 750 fits / 752 bumps; height 558 fits / 560 bumps. Verified on non-fitted
  values. (An Image/drop-zone node with no content extent -> 720x486.) The 750x558 fit-box aspect is
  ~1.344; not a clean named preset, but it is a MEASURED, reproducible FCP law (valid RULE-2.0 decode
  via controlled probe).
STATUS: DECODED but NOT IMPLEMENTED. Implementing requires the engine to compute the union content
bbox-span across all shapes/media and select 720x486 vs 1920x1080 accordingly — a real subsystem for a
corner that affects ZERO shipped transitions (all 65 have complete sceneSettings). Deferred as
disproportionate. The engine keeps the historical 1920x1080 default.
WORKFLOW: this is why sceneSettings-stripped re-minimizes (Reflection/Center/Diagonal) collapse to the
canvas corner. To make progress on those transitions' REAL bugs, diagnose the EXISTING minimized cases
(sceneSettings intact) or the full transition, not the width/height/PAR-stripped re-minimize.

## Movements/Reflection existing repro — B swings OFF-SCREEN vs FCP centered (2026-07-26 diagnosis)
The existing Movements__Reflection minimized repro (full sceneSettings, both render 1920x1080) diverges
hard in the tail (f20-23: engine BLACK, FCP photo B [100,115,147]). ROOT: Transition B is a card with
Position Z=960, Anchor-Point Z=960, Rotation Y animating 90deg->0 (swing-in), driven by a Rig Behavior
(factoryID 11) whose Snapshots set the hidden driver's Position, + LinkPos/LinkAnchor coupling Z. The
engine projects B's worldX to 315(f10)->1606(f18)->1891(f22) — i.e. B slides far RIGHT and off the
1920 frame as it rotates face-on, so the tail renders black. FCP keeps B CENTERED (face-on) at the end.
So the engine's anchor-Z pivot + Y-rotation under perspective yields the wrong screen-X translation:
rotating a card about an anchor offset in +Z (the "spine" hinge at Z=960) should swing it into a
CENTERED face-on pose, but the engine's projection walks it off to the right. This is the known deep
Reflection 3D-rig residual (anchor-Z pivot projection): the pivot math (rotate about a Z-offset anchor
then perspective-project) is not matching FCP. NEEDS the anchor-Z pivot + camera projection decoded
(how FCP composes anchorZ into the rotation pivot vs the position). Deep 3D-transform work; captured for
a focused session. (MEMORY's earlier "Reflection perspective focal mismatch 24.77 dB" is this family.)

## Movements/Reflection FIXED 12.84 -> 25.38 dB — anchor-Z convention + link-rotationY sign (2026-07-26) a45568e, 347a0fe
Two decoded 3D-transform fixes to the core evaluator matrix (buildTransformMatrix):
 1. a45568e — anchor Z term is +ancZ, not -ancZ. Motion's anchor Z uses the same "+Z toward camera"
    convention as position Z (which maps to world -posZ). The inner T(-anchor) must therefore use +ancZ
    (subtracting a toward-camera anchor at world -960 is +960). With the old -ancZ, a card whose
    posZ==anchorZ (hinging about its own Z-offset spine) placed the pivot at world Z=-1920 instead of
    cancelling → its centre swung to world X≈+960 at edge-on and projected OFF-SCREEN → BLACK tail.
 2. 347a0fe — negate LINK-DRIVEN rotationY (ov.has('rotY')), mirroring the existing link-driven
    rotationZ negation (Motion's link resolver inverts the sign vs our matrix). Without it the group
    hinged about the screen-RIGHT edge (mirror of FCP's left-spine hinge).
DECODED on Movements/Reflection Transition B (group posZ=anchorZ=960 + LinkRot Y 0→-90° off the hidden
Color Solid). FCP hinges B about the screen-LEFT spine: content grows x[0,775]→[0,1614]→[0,1894],
settling centred/face-on on photo B. Before: engine swung B off-canvas right (tail BLACK, |D| ~120).
After both fixes: B hinges left and settles centred, per-frame |D| 66-91 → 10-19; mean 12.84 → 25.38 dB.
GENERAL (not slug-keyed): both are core transform-matrix corrections; the anchor-Z convention + link-
rotationY sign apply to any 3D-hinge transition — likely also helps Movements/Swing (door-hinge family).
Golden 2123/0 throughout. RESIDUAL: mid-swing |D| ~13-19 (finer swing-arc/reflection-floor geometry).

## Movements/Reflection FIXED 12.84 -> 25.38 dB — two decoded 3D-transform sign fixes (2026-07-26) a45568e, 347a0fe
The existing Reflection repro (Transition B = card with posZ=anchorZ=960 + a LinkRot Y 0→-90° off the
hidden Color-Solid driver, hinging about its Z-offset "spine") diverged HARD (tail BLACK, |D|~120): the
engine swung B OFF-SCREEN RIGHT while FCP hinges B about the screen-LEFT spine and settles it centred on
photo B. TWO decoded evaluator/buildTransformMatrix fixes:
 1. a45568e — anchor Z term = +ancZ (not -ancZ). Motion's anchor Z shares the "+Z toward camera"
    convention as position Z (which maps to world -posZ); the SUBTRACTED anchor must therefore be +ancZ.
    Old -ancZ placed the rotation pivot at world Z=-1920 for a posZ==anchorZ hinge, so the card centre
    swung to world X≈+960 at edge-on → off-canvas. +ancZ cancels at face-on (centre→world 0) and hinges
    correctly. (Fixed the off-screen/BLACK tail: f22 |D| 119.9→15.6, f23 121.1→8.7 → 15.40 dB.)
 2. 347a0fe — negate LINK-DRIVEN rotationY (ov.has('rotY')), mirroring the existing link-driven
    rotationZ sign inversion (Motion's Link resolver produces the opposite sign vs our RotY basis).
    Without it the group hinged about the screen-RIGHT edge (mirror of FCP). Negating flips it to the
    left spine, matching FCP's content growth x[0,775]→[0,1614]→[0,1894]. (15.40 → 25.38 dB.)
BOTH are GENERAL core-transform corrections (not slug-keyed): the anchor-Z convention + link-rotationY
sign apply to any 3D-hinge transition — likely also lift Movements/Swing (door-hinge family). Golden
2123/0 throughout. RESIDUAL: mid-swing f13-f18 |D|~20 (finer swing-arc scale / reflective-floor plane).

## Movements__Swing — RESOLVED to 40.50 dB (2026-07-26) — commits d9b285b + 15a6a8f
The full door-reveal mechanism now works. Four decoded fixes:
1. (d9b285b) Rig Behavior with an ABSENT Widget object is INERT — keeps the affected channel's
   AUTHORED value. The minimizer stripped the Anchor/Direction Widget scenenodes; FCP-headless renders
   the stripped .motr identically to the full transition (absent-widget rig → no-op). The engine had
   defaulted the missing widgetValue to 0 → snapshot[0] (Opacity 1), wrongly forcing the authored-hidden
   'Right' doors visible. Skip rigs whose widgetId ∉ widgetValues (which only holds REAL widgets).
2. (15a6a8f) DISABLED TRANSITION-SOURCE FEEDS CLONES. resolveCloneImage now returns imageA/imageB for a
   Clone of a disabled Transition A/B leaf (in an ENABLED group), while a clone whose path passes through
   a DISABLED GROUP (disabled ancestor, tracked in new RenderContext.disabledSubtreeIds) is still culled.
   Preserves 3dr_v7 (43.81); fixes Swing's door showing B.
3. (15a6a8f) LINK-DRIVEN-ROT sign separation. New Transform.__linkDrivenRot set carries ONLY genuinely
   link-driven rotY/rotZ (Reflection LinkRot, Switch rotZ) + REAL A/B drop-zone static pre-folds
   (Reflection B +pi/2 → -pi/2). A CLONE's static fold (Swing Clone-B -pi/2) is NOT sign-flipped. Fixes
   Swing's door composing to +180° off-screen. Reflection 25.38 preserved.
4. (15a6a8f) EDGE-ON CULL (perspective.isEdgeOnQuad). A clone card within EPS (0.02) of edge-on
   (|face-normal·viewAxis|≈0) has ~zero true area → FCP renders nothing. Removes the ~440px half-bright
   wedge the receding edge-on door projected during the held-shut first third. NOT back-face culling
   (Reflection's mirrored cards reach normal.z=-1 and draw; min |normal.z| visible = 0.116).
RESIDUAL (worst ~12 dB at f9-f11): mid-reveal foreshortening — FCP concentrates the opening door face
into a narrower/brighter sliver than the engine's receding-card projection (engine reveal edge tracks
FCP to within ~70px: f9 736 vs 805, f10 1302 vs 1343, f12 1919 exact). A finer grazing-projection issue.

## Movements__Swing — SHIPPED 31.43→40.50 dB (2026-07-26): door reveals B via 3 decoded fixes (15a6a8f + d9b285b)
The double-doors (Clones of the disabled Transition B) failed to reveal B: engine rendered BLACK the whole
transition where FCP swings the doors open to show B. Root-caused to THREE distinct engine bugs, all fixed:
1. RIG BEHAVIOR WITH ABSENT WIDGET IS INERT (d9b285b, links.ts applyRigBehaviors + filter-overrides.ts):
   the minimizer stripped the Anchor/Direction Widget scenenodes (referenced only by <publishSettings>),
   yet FCP-headless renders the stripped .motr IDENTICALLY. FCP resolves an absent-widget rig to a no-op,
   leaving the affected channel's AUTHORED value. Engine had defaulted missing widgetValue to 0 → snapshot[0]
   (Opacity 1), wrongly forcing the authored-hidden 'Right' group visible. Skip rigs whose widgetId ∉ widgetValues.
2. DISABLED TRANSITION-SOURCE FEEDS CLONES (masks.ts resolveCloneImage + context.ts/index.ts disabledSubtreeIds):
   a Clone of the disabled 'Transition B' SOURCE leaf must still render B media (disabling a transition source
   hides only its own draw; its whole purpose is to be cloned). NEW rule: resolve transitionA/B media before the
   own-enabled bail, but FIRST cull clones whose source is inside a DISABLED GROUP (disabledSubtreeIds) — keeps
   3dr_v7's dead-chain cull (43.81) while letting Swing's disabled-leaf-in-enabled-group feed its doors.
3. LINK-DRIVEN-ROT SIGN SEPARATION (types.ts __linkDrivenRot; links.ts; evaluator/index.ts): split the rotY/rotZ
   sign-flip off __overrideChannels (which also carries retime-bypass static folds) onto a dedicated
   __linkDrivenRot set. LINK-driven rotations AND REAL A/B drop-zone static pre-folds (Reflection B +π/2→−π/2)
   flip; a CLONE's static fold (Swing Clone-B −π/2) does NOT. Without this Swing's door composed to +180°.
4. EDGE-ON CULL (perspective.ts isEdgeOnQuad + index.ts clone path): a clone card held near-edge-on
   (|face-normal.z|<0.02) has ~zero true area → FCP BLACK; engine projected a spurious ~440px wedge. Cull it.
   NOT back-face culling (Reflection's mirrored cards reach normal.z=−1 and draw; min |nz| visible = 0.116).
VERIFIED: Swing 31.43→40.50, Reflection 25.38 (unchanged), 3dr_v7 43.81 (unchanged), golden 2123/0, tsc clean.
LESSON: an all-BLACK render can score deceptively high on PSNR-mean (perfect on FCP's black frames); the real
metric is whether the mechanism matches FCP. Fixing the mechanism (door reveals B) is the win even if a finer
residual (mid-reveal foreshortening f9-f11, ~40px reveal-edge lag) remains.

## Stylized__Center (13.02 dB full / min _t_center_v2 52.91) — NEXT TARGET (2026-07-26)
Full Center diverges two ways (fct/minimized/Stylized__Center, 6-node minimized):
- f3-f9: engine renders the "Right full" gradient SHAPE (grey [28→98]) as its animating Crop opens
  (Left 444→0, Top 829→0), while FCP is BLACK. The shape should be HIDDEN/masked, not drawn.
- f12-f21: engine composites the gradient shape OVER Transition B → [150,160,180] (too bright);
  FCP shows only settled B [100,116,148]. Shape must be GONE by f12.
ROOT MECHANISM (from full Center.motr): the real transition uses <group name="Shapes for image mask"
id=554019971> — an ELABORATE animated boolean-mask assembly ("leaf mask helper", "Final Bottom Mask",
"Trans B Mask 1/2/3", "Little full" with triangle/leaf/box masks) referenced by the Comp layer's
Image Mask (Invert=1, Mask Source=554019971). This animated center-cutout mask reveals B while hiding
the panel shapes. The minimizer STRIPS 554019971, so the minimal _t_center_v2 (52.91) only shows a
trivial f0 mask-timing edge (engine grey [20,20,20] vs FCP black at f0; matches f4+). The REAL fix
needs the animated Image-Mask-shape-assembly subsystem: parse the "Shapes for image mask" group,
rasterize its (cropped, animated) union as the Comp's inverted image mask, and clip the panel shapes
so they vanish as the center cutout grows. This is a NEW subsystem (animated multi-shape boolean image
mask), not a quick fix. Also: the panel "Right full" is a Shape with a GRADIENT fill — verify the
gradient decode matches (Center's panels may be tinted, not raw B).

## Stylized__Slide_In — SHIPPED 12.96→21.45 dB (2026-07-26): removed backwards motion-path-mask Y-negation (2e5a99c)
The sliding gradient panel rendered in the WRONG half of the frame. Root cause: the motion-path-mask
Y-negation added in 7acc747 (negate posY/ancY for a <mask> lifted off a generator/image leaf) was
correct against the buildTransformMatrix Y convention AT THAT TIME, but the later anchor-Z toward-camera
+ Y-down toolchain fixes inverted its effect. PROBED on the "Rounded rect down" mask (Position Y=-1229,
Anchor Y=-606, Scale Y=0.912): RAW values give m13 = -1229 + 0.912·606 = -676 (centered) → band at
screen y[0,416] EXACTLY matching FCP; the negation gave +676 → y[664,1079] (exact mirror about centre).

underlying convention. When a position looks mirror-flipped, re-derive the expected transform value from
first principles (posY + scaleY·(-ancY)) against the CURRENT matrix, don't trust an old negation comment.

## Wipes__Diagonal — ROOT CAUSE ISOLATED (2026-07-26): mask write-on reveal LAGS FCP by ~2.5×
Minimized Diagonal (Color Theme widget stripped) → FCP reveals the SOLID GREY Background [~195 =
srgbToLinear(227)] over A via the diagonal "Animated mask" sweep. (The FULL case is GREEN via the
Color Theme; minimized is grey — so the earlier "green not composited" note was the FULL case.) The
ENGINE renders the grey Background correctly BUT reveals it far too SLOWLY:
  grey-reveal fraction  f3   f6   f9   f12  f15
    ENGINE               0%   0%   28%  66%  97%
    FCP                  0%  16%   81% 100% 100%
So FCP completes the wipe by ~f12 (sceneT 0.567) while the engine drags it to ~f15+. Two compounding causes:
1. OFFSET DELAY: the "Animated mask" has offset=0.300s; the write-on envelope uses curveTime=timeSec-offset,
   so at f6 (0.284s) curveTime<0 → reveals NOTHING, while FCP is already 16% revealed. The offset shift
   delays the sweep start ~0.3s past where FCP starts.
2. SWEEP-SPEED / COVERAGE: even accounting for start, the engine's write-on envelope reaches 100% frame
   coverage at the sweep MIDPOINT much later than FCP. The mask Position curve is authored [0.300s→1.068s]
   (≈ endSec 1.1345) but FCP reaches full coverage by ~0.567s (half) — FCP appears to COMPRESS the authored
   mask-sweep timeline to ~half the span (same family as the Light Sweep animationEndSec≠span compression),
   OR the mask quad (aspectRatio 1.5, feather 300) is LARGER in FCP so it covers the frame earlier in its sweep.
NEXT: decode whether FCP (a) drops the mask's offset shift for the write-on start, and (b) compresses the
mask Position curve to [~0, endSec/2] (timeline compression) or uses a larger mask quad. Needs a controlled
probe of the mask sweep vs FCP reveal-front position per frame. Genuine shipped divergence (full 18.72 dB).

## Wipes__Diagonal — PROBE RESULTS (2026-07-26): offset shift + non-monotonic write-on
Measured FCP reveal precisely: grey Background reveal ramps f5→f11 (sceneT [0.236, 0.520]), midpoint f7.5.
Engine (offset-shifted, curveTime=timeSec-0.300) reveals [~0.36,0.71], midpoint ~f13.5 — ~6 frames LATE
(the lag ≈ the 0.300s offset). PROBE (removed offset, curveTime=timeSec, then REVERTED): reveal jumps too
EARLY (f5=61% vs FCP 4%) AND then RETREATS (f11=70%, f12=48%) — the write-on envelope is NOT holding
monotonic when fed raw time. So BOTH the offset-shift (too late) and raw-time (too early + non-monotonic)
are wrong; FCP is in between: reveal completes at sceneT 0.520 = when the mask curve (keyframes scene-abs
[0.300,1.068]) has swept ~29% (mask center → (-132,-969), the feather-300 quad's soft edge just clears the
frame). TWO fixes needed, decoded together:
  (a) The mask Position keyframes are SCENE-ABSOLUTE (0.300 == the offset == first-kf time), so the
      curveTime=timeSec-offset shift DOUBLE-COUNTS and delays the sweep ~0.3s. Correct: evaluate the curve
      at raw scene time (no offset shift) for a mask whose first keyframe time ≈ its offset.
  (b) The write-on envelope must be TRULY MONOTONIC: with raw time it retreated (f11 70%→f12 48%), meaning
      the K-sample union [0,curveTime] loses coverage as the quad exits — likely because the K samples are
      spread over [0,curveTime] (re-spaced each frame) rather than accumulating a fixed dense history, so
      late frames under-sample the early (entering) positions. Fix: sample at a FIXED cadence from mask-
      start so the envelope only grows. Verify reveal ramp matches FCP f5→f11 exactly, and re-check the
      other write-on slug (Color_Panels static mask must stay unaffected — it has no sweep).
This is the concrete next-iteration task; needs the write-on envelope reworked + offset-shift scoped off
for scene-absolute-keyframe masks. Full Diagonal genuine divergence 18.72 dB.

## Wipes__Diagonal — KEY CORRECTION (2026-07-26): reveal is SCALE-driven, not just Position
The "Animated mask" reveal is driven PRIMARILY by a SCALE animation, not the Position sweep:
  Scale X: 2.088 → 2.088 (constant), Scale Y: 0.686 → 5.941 (grows 8.7×!) over LOCAL times [-0.200, 0.567].
  Position: (-1046,-2129) → (2105,1870) over [0.300, 1.068].
The FIRST Scale keyframe is at NEGATIVE local time (-0.200), which only makes sense in a LOCAL time frame
(so the curves ARE local, and offset=0.300 anchors local-zero at scene 0.300). With curveTime=timeSec-0.300:
at scene 0.236 → curveTime -0.064 → ScaleY≈1.62 (already growing) → reveal begins ≈ FCP's f5 start. The
mask STRETCHES (ScaleY 0.69→5.94) to cover the frame by curveTime≈0.567 (scene 0.867 offset-shifted, but
FCP completes at 0.520). So the write-on `swept` test (checks TRANSLATION >8px only) MISSES that the reveal
is scale-dominated, and the K sub-time sampling + trailing-band were tuned for a translating quad.
REVISED next-iteration plan: the write-on envelope must account for the SCALE-Y growth (the quad expands to
fill), and the offset/timeline must be re-derived so the ScaleY-driven coverage completes at scene 0.520 (not
0.867). The negative-time Scale keyframe + offset interaction is the crux. This fully explains why both the
offset-shifted (too late) and raw-time (too early) probes failed — neither models the scale growth correctly.
Genuine shipped divergence (18.72 dB); needs a scale-aware write-on rework decoded against headless.

## Wipes__Diagonal — KEY FINDING (2026-07-26): the reveal is driven by mask SCALE-Y growth, not Position
The "Animated mask" has a SCALE animation (not just Position): Scale Y grows 0.686 → 5.941 (~8.6×) over
scene-absolute time [-0.200s, 0.567s], Scale X constant ~2.088. The Y-scale growth is what EXPANDS the
feathered quad to cover the frame; it COMPLETES at t=0.567s ≈ FCP's reveal-complete (0.520). The Scale
keyframes are SCENE-ABSOLUTE (negative start -0.200s, end 0.567s aligned to the transition), NOT local.
The engine's curveTime=timeSec-offset(0.300) shift evaluates the Scale curve 0.3s LATE, so at t=0.520 the
mask is only ~59% grown (scaleY~3.8 not 5.94) → under-covers → the ~6-frame reveal lag. THE OFFSET SHIFT
IS THE BUG (curves are scene-absolute; the shift double-counts the 0.300 first-Position-kf/offset).
BUT naive offset-removal overshoots the START (probe f5=61% vs FCP 4%) because (i) Position holds off-frame
until 0.300 while ScaleY is already ~3.7 at t=0.236, and (ii) the trailing-wipe-out band + write-on K-sample
spread then make it non-monotonic (retreat f11→f12). CORRECT FIX (next iteration): evaluate the mask's
Position/Scale/Rotation curves at RAW scene time (drop the offset shift for scene-absolute-kf masks —
detect via negative or offset-aligned keyframe times), AND re-tune the write-on leading-union + trailing-
band against the HEADLESS oracle so the reveal ramp matches FCP f5→f11 exactly without the retreat. The
current offset shift + trailing band were tuned on GUI-GT (not headless) — that is why they mismatch the
headless reveal. Needs the SCALE curve included in the write-on sub-time sampling (it currently samples
Position via buildTransformMatrix at sub-times, which DOES include scale — verify the scale grows across
the K samples). Full Diagonal 18.72 dB; this is the single highest-value wipe-family fix.

## Wipes__Diagonal — PROBE2 (offset fully removed): reveal + wipeout BOTH too early → coupled geom+timing
With curveTime=timeSec (no offset shift), the engine reveal ramps f3=34→f8=98% then RETREATS f11=70→f15=2%
(trailing-band wipes out early because Position sweeps the quad off-frame sooner in raw time). FCP: reveal
f5→f11, HOLD 100% f11-f15, wipeout f18-f21. So neither offset-on (reveal f9-f15, too late) nor offset-off
(reveal f3-f8 + early wipeout) matches — FCP is BETWEEN. Root: the engine's scaled+feathered mask quad
reaches FULL frame COVERAGE at scaleY~4.9 (curveTime 0.42) whereas FCP needs the mask fully grown
(scaleY 5.94 @ curve 0.567) → the engine's effective mask footprint (quad size × feather) is TOO LARGE, so
it covers the frame at a lower scale/earlier than FCP. CONCLUSION: this is a COUPLED geometry+timing bug —
fixing the offset alone can't match FCP; the mask's effective coverage-vs-scaleY curve must ALSO match
(likely the feather=300 or the quad aspect/size is over-covering). Needs a joint decode: (1) verify the
mask quad+feather rasterized footprint vs FCP's reveal front at matched scale, (2) then set the timeline so
Scale-Y-full (curve 0.567) lands at FCP's full-reveal (scene 0.520) and the Position-driven wipeout lands at
FCP's f18-21. HIGH RISK to the shared trailing-band (tuned for other write-on slugs) — do as a dedicated
rework with per-slug write-on regression checks. LEFT AS-IS this session (offset version committed); fully
documented for a focused next attempt. Full Diagonal 18.72 dB.

UNCHANGED (f12 69% vs baseline 70%; score 19.01 vs 19.13, noise). So the feather geometry is NOT the lever;
the reveal LAG is dominated by the write-on/offset TIMING (engine reveals f7-f15, FCP f5-f11; wipe-out
engine f18=56% vs FCP 13%). Reverted the feather change (didn't fix the verified case, slightly noisy-down).

delaying the Scale-Y-driven reveal ~2 frames AND compressing the wipe-out ~5 frames late. The correct fix is
a joint re-decode of the write-on timeline (drop offset for scene-absolute-kf masks; include Scale-Y in the
envelope; re-tune leading-union + trailing-band vs the HEADLESS oracle). Feather is faithful-but-latent here.

the CELL's authored RGB gradient colour (warm) and the correct spatial extent for Drop_In (broad splash as

simulate the ballistic warm droplets for Drop_In. This is particle-sim tuning in an EXISTING subsystem
(emitter-sim.ts), not a missing feature. The B-bounce offset fix (319ab03, f2 warm) stands; the splash is
the dominant remaining ~12 dB divergence. Verified this session: golden 2123/0, tsc clean, all pushed.

## Movements__Drop_In — CORRECTION: warm = A showing through, NOT particle color (2026-07-26)
The particle RGB1 color is [1.0,0.94,0.95] = near-WHITE (not warm orange). So FCP's warm mid-transition
mean [142,114,104] at f6 is the A PHOTO showing through (+ near-white splash on top), NOT a warm particle.
So the REAL divergence: FCP keeps ~63% of the frame showing warm A at f6, but the engine's full-frame B
(bounced to ty=-156, scale 1.0) covers the frame (1% warm). Even with the correct B-bounce timing, a
full-frame B at ty=-156 covers the top ~2/3; FCP shows more A. So either (a) B is BEHIND A / A composites
over B during the drop (z-order), or (b) B's drop card is CLIPPED/smaller than full-frame in FCP, or (c)
FCP's B bounce reaches center LATER than the engine. This is a compositing/z-order/geometry question needing
pixel-level analysis of where B covers vs A across frames. The near-white splash particles are a SECONDARY
overlay (the impact dust). PRIORITY for next Drop_In session: determine why FCP's A stays visible at f6
(z-order of B-drop vs A base, or B card extent) — that is the ~12 dB lever, not the particle color.

## Movements__Drop_In — z-order + B-drop timing deep-dive (2026-07-26)
Confirmed paint order: Motion "first-in-list = top/foreground" (compositor index.ts:1604). Parsed tree has
Transition B at idx3 (foreground) and Transition A at idx4 (background) — so B paints ON TOP of A. Engine
at f6: B full-frame at ty=-156 (covering) → shows B-blue. FCP at f6 shows warm A (63% warm). So FCP's
foreground B must still be OFF-FRAME at f6 (letting background A show), i.e. FCP's B bounce is SLOWER than
the engine's raw-curve evaluation. The B Position-Y bounce (719→−416→…→0, local [0,1.268s]) with the
offset shift gives ty=−154 at f6 (B covering) — matches the raw curve but NOT FCP. So FCP RETIMES B's
position bounce (B has a Retime Value curve frame 1→46 over [0,1.502s]); the bounce likely plays on the
RETIMED media clock, not raw scene time, so B stays high (off-frame) much longer. NEXT: evaluate B's
Position bounce at the RETIMED time (map scene→retimed via the Retime Value curve) so B drops in slowly
matching FCP; then the near-white splash particles overlay correctly. This is B-drop retiming (couples the
Retime Value curve into the position-curve evaluation) + the particle splash. Deep timing decode; deferred.
The B-bounce OFFSET fix (319ab03) stands (correct local-frame anchoring); the RETIME coupling is the next layer.

## Movements__Drop_In — DEEPER: B arrives too fast (retime-vs-local-time on the bounce) (2026-07-26)
Paint order confirmed (Motion "first=top"): Transition B (idx3) is ON TOP of Transition A (idx4, bottom).
So when B's full-frame card covers the frame, the engine correctly shows B. The divergence: at f6 the engine
has B bounced to ty=-156 (covering frame → blue), but FCP still shows warm A → FCP's B is still OFF-frame
at f6. So FCP's B drop is SLOWER than the engine's. B carries a Retime Value (frame 1→46 over local
[0,1.502s]); the hypothesis is that B's POSITION bounce (keyframe times 0→1.268s) is indexed in RETIMED-
FRAME space, so the bounce plays out stretched by the retime — arriving at centre only near the end (~f18-22,
matching FCP's late B arrival). The engine evaluates the animated position curve at RAW local time
(resolveWithRetime → evaluateCurve(value, timeSec) for keyframed curves), so B arrives ~3× too fast. NEXT:
decode whether a drop-zone's transform curves are indexed by the RETIMED frame (retimeValue) rather than
raw local time — if so, evaluate the bounce at the retime-mapped time. This + the near-white impact
particles (secondary) are the Drop_In levers. The particle RGB is near-white [1,0.94,0.95] (NOT the warm —
warm is A showing through), so the splash is secondary; the PRIMARY lever is B's drop SPEED (retime-indexed
position). Substantial retime-decode task. B-bounce offset fix (319ab03) stands (fixed the START).

## Empty-media clip placeholder — SHIPPED 2026-07-27 (7f5c56e)
DECODED (controlled headless probes) + implemented: a footage <clip> with NO pathURL/relativeURL/name AND
no <missingWidth>/<missingHeight> is TRULY EMPTY → FCP renders a missing-media PLACEHOLDER, not the user's
A/B media. Two variants, flipped SOLELY by <sceneSettings> presence (probe-proven: adding sceneSettings to
an empty-media scene flips red→black):
  • no <sceneSettings> (DV canvas): solid RED [204,0,0] (fixed placeholder; NOT the Fill Color — injecting
    a green Fill Color left it red).
  • with <sceneSettings>: transparent (black).
The engine's A/B order-fallback previously bound empty clips to imageA → painted the warm test photo.
FIX: parser marks empty clips 'E' (excluded from A/B fallback); ImageSource {type:'empty'} → red-if-
noSceneSettings else null. A clip WITH <missingWidth>/<missingHeight> is a REAL dimensioned unfilled drop
zone (Reflection _t_refl_np Type=2 1200×1200) → stays A/B (NOT 'E').
RESULTS: _t_3dr 10.13→63.70, _t_upover 7.09→18.48, _t_clonespin3 7.09→18.48, _t_refl_np 13.36 (preserved),
golden 2123/0. _t_diag_noprotect 19.26→18.03 (red now correct but FCP draws it as a SMALL centred ~281×255
card over black, not full-frame — a fixed-size missing-media card, finer residual). Shipped transitions
